Titles come only from the supplied source pages; publisher, library and critical sources establish identity, purpose and assessment evidence. Publisher promotion is not independent proof of quality.

## Method and reading priority

Fiction and primarily instructional or specialist technical works are removed before vetoes. All remaining non-vetoed works are scored, including poor fits. E, S and D use their own quadratic profiles; the other seven criteria remain linear. T11 and T12 are separate thematic deductions. H13 uses the assessed historical endpoint, P14 the original production year, and O15 one verified Amazon rating aggregate. Unknown Amazon counts receive the required zero-deduction fallback, not a claim of popularity. Calculations and sorting retain full precision; displayed values use two decimals.

Verdicts: Recommend ≥85; Consider ≥75; Selective ≥65; Low priority ≥50; Poor fit <50. Confidence describes evidence strength, not statistical probability. Reader aggregates are separate from the quality score. A missing rating is shown as unavailable; no rating is invented for Book Marks, LibraryThing, StoryGraph, Goodreads or Amazon.

The highest-ranked eligible works are *A Mind At Play* (71.42), *The Wright Brothers* (69.22), *The Intel Trinity* (66.46), *Andy Grove: The Life and Times of an American* (65.04), *Edison* (64.84). The lowest-ranked eligible works are *Napoleon* (10.11), *Twelve Against The Gods* (5.16), *The Confessions* (-3.82); low scores do not remove them from the recommendation ledger.

| #AskGaryVee — Gary Vaynerchuk | 2 · high | A question-and-answer business and social-media advice guide. [1](https://www.amazon.com.au/dp/1984878506) [2](https://openlibrary.org/works/OL20641963W) Source entries: [A Woman Makes a Plan Maye Musk](https://glasp.co/books/p/a-woman-makes-a-plan-advice-for-a-lifetime-of-adventure-beauty-and-success). |
| Can't Hurt Me — David Goggins | 2 · high | Change from prior recommended q0925. The personal narrative is organized to teach a self-improvement method, with ten challenges requiring the reader to apply it. The prior low score did not apply the now-explicit instructional hard gate. No new score is assigned. [1](https://davidgoggins.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/05/CantHurtMe-Intro.pdf) Prior status: recommended; [prior assessment](analysis_changedmind_261017_2105.md#c106). Source entries: [Can't Hurt Me David Goggins](https://glasp.co/books/p/can-t-hurt-me-master-your-mind-and-defy-the-odds). |
| Dare To Fly — Martha McSally | 2 · moderate | The publisher explicitly frames the memoir around teaching readers to overcome fear, transform doubt and perform under pressure. The life episodes support a self-improvement program, making instruction the main purpose. [1](https://www.amazon.com.au/dp/0062996282) [2](https://openlibrary.org/works/OL20859629W) Source entries: [Dare To Fly Martha McSally](https://glasp.co/books/p/dare-to-fly-simple-lessons-in-never-giving-up). |
| Fear and Loathing in Las Vegas — Hunter S. Thompson | 1 · moderate | A novel using a fictionalized persona and invented or transformed events within the gonzo-journalism style. Autobiographical origins and the publisher’s journalism label do not make it a reliable nonfiction travel memoir. [1](https://www.penguinrandomhouse.com/books/178187/fear-and-loathing-in-las-vegas-and-other-american-stories-by-hunter-s-thompson/) [2](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fear_and_Loathing_in_Las_Vegas) Source entries: [Fear and Loathing in Las Vegas Hunter S. Thompson](https://glasp.co/books/p/fear-and-loathing-in-las-vegas-a-savage-journey-to-the-heart-of-the-american-dream). |
| Lessons — Gisele Bündchen | 2 · moderate | A personal-development book structured around lessons for living, relationships, wellbeing and work, supported by autobiographical stories. Its main purpose is to guide the reader’s own behavior. [1](https://www.amazon.com.au/dp/052553864X) [2](https://openlibrary.org/works/OL19754798W) Source entries: [Lessons Gisele Bundchen](https://glasp.co/books/p/lessons-my-path-to-a-meaningful-life). |
| Life Is What You Make It — Peter Buffett | 2 · moderate | A practical self-development account organized to guide choices, values and a satisfying career. The personal stories function as lessons for the reader rather than a sustained explanatory biography. [1](https://www.amazon.com.au/dp/B0036S4B9Y) Source entries: [Life Is What You Make It Peter Buffett](https://glasp.co/books/p/life-is-what-you-make-it-find-your-own-path-to-fulfillment). |
| On Writing — Stephen King | 2 · high | A mixed memoir and writing course whose central Toolbox and On Writing sections teach grammar, composition, drafting and revision. The publisher identifies the main value as practical tools of the writer’s craft; autobiographical framing does not override rule 2. [1](https://www.simonandschuster.com/books/On-Writing/Stephen-King/9781982159375) Source entries: [On Writing Stephen King](https://glasp.co/books/p/on-writing-a-memoir-of-the-craft). |
| Self-Made Success — Shaan Patel | 2 · high | An explicit success blueprint organized into strategies for wealth, entrepreneurship and personal achievement. The main structure teaches the reader methods and actions. [1](https://www.amazon.com.au/dp/1532854773) [2](https://openlibrary.org/works/OL26147006W) Source entries: [Self-Made Success Shaan Patel](https://glasp.co/books/p/self-made-success-ivy-league-shark-tank-entrepreneur-reveals-48-secret-strategies-to-live-happier-healthier-and-wealthier). |
| The Almanack of Naval Ravikant — Eric Jorgenson | 2 · high | The author’s full contents organize the text as instruction in building wealth, making decisions and learning happiness. Interview excerpts and aphorisms teach personal methods; the marketing claim that it is not a how-to book does not override its instructional purpose and structure. [1](https://www.navalmanack.com/almanack-of-naval-ravikant/table-of-contents) Source entries: [The Almanack of Naval Ravikant Eric Jorgenson](https://glasp.co/books/p/the-almanack-of-naval-ravikant-a-guide-to-wealth-and-happiness). |
| Trailblazer — Marc Benioff; Monica Langley | 2 · moderate | A practical values-based company-culture guide built around the founder’s examples and best practices. Its main promise is to show managers how to make firms platforms for change. Treat the memoir material as support for this instructional purpose. [1](https://www.amazon.com.au/dp/1984825194) [2](https://openlibrary.org/works/OL21343410W) Source entries: [Trailblazer Marc Benioff](https://glasp.co/books/p/trailblazer-the-power-of-business-as-the-greatest-platform-for-change). |

<a id="g0032"></a>
### A Mind At Play

**Jimmy Soni; Rob Goodman; original production 2017. 71.42/100; Selective. Score confidence: low.**

A general-reader biography of Claude Shannon. Research habits, technical play and institutional freedom provide payload; the scientific biography exception applies. It does not primarily teach information theory.

E/A credit documented biography, with limited independent reception reviewed here. S/U/M/F connect mathematics, engineering and research practice. D/R/N/P credit focused durable explanation.

<a id="g0044"></a>
### Best Evidence

**David Lifton; original production 1981. Veto V1; counterfactual -16.43/100; not recommended. Score confidence: low.**

V1, moderate confidence: the central body-alteration theory depends on speculative reconciliation of inconsistent witness memories without direct evidence of the alleged operation. The archived contemporary report explicitly identifies this evidential gap. This is nonfiction advocacy of a claim, not a novel.

E/M/P are sharply reduced for the unsupported causal bridge; S/U/F are minimal. A credits investigation rather than validation. D/R are low, and N retains some narrative organization.

## Coverage and status audit

All 146 captured source records map to the work identities in the inventory. Every eligible non-vetoed work above is included in RECOMMENDED_BOOKS.md; every vetoed work is included only in VETOED_BOOKS.md; every hard-excluded work is included only in EXCLUDED_BOOKS.md. Earlier analysis files are preserved as history. [Compiled records](data/glasp_261019/compiled.json) retain source occurrences, raw attainments, contributions and deductions.
